Price(09/14)：323.5

上銀 26Q2 受惠於產品漲價效益顯現及 UTR 提升，單季毛利率攀
升至 34.17%。單季合併營收達 75.08 億元，QoQ 17.70% 、YoY 
26.71%。營業利益與稅後淨利分別大幅成長至 10.34 億元與 8.48
億元，帶動單季 EPS 攀升至 2.40 元。 
展望後市， 研究部對上銀營收表現維持正向看待 。 目前終端需求持
續暢旺，產品交期維持高位，線性滑軌能見度約 4 至 5 個月，滾
珠螺桿最長可達 6 個月 。 儘管地緣政治因素推升鋼材 、 包材與潤滑
油等原物料價格 ， 但公司具備彈性定價能力且維持多元採購策略，
漲價效益足以抵銷成本上揚。伴隨26Q3 完整反映漲價產品銷售，
預估單季營收達 80.92 億元，QoQ 7.77%，YoY 34.97%，EPS 上
看 2.81 元。放眼 2026 至 2027 年，亞洲半導體需求穩固、歐洲軍
工與航太產業接棒成為新動能 ， 整體訂單需求無虞 。 預估上銀2026
年與 2027 年營收將分別達 296.90 億元與 329.36 億元 ， 維持雙位
數年增幅，YoY 分別為 22.37%與 10.93%。在營運規模提升帶動
下，預估 2026 年、2027 年 EPS 將分別達 9.28 元與 11.87 元。 
評價方面，上銀過去五年 PER 主要集中於 20 倍至 40 倍區間。考
量公司營收與獲利將呈現雙位數成長上升趨勢，產業結構持續上
行，研究部以2027 年預估 EPS 11.87 元作為評價基礎，並給予歷
史區間中緣偏上之 35x 本益比，目標價為 420 元，給予「買進」
之投資建議。  
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
上銀科技(2049)成立於 1989 年，主要從事線性滑軌(GW)、滾
螺桿(BS)、工業機器人、機器手臂、迴轉工作台、減速機、精
密軸承及醫療設備之研究開發、設計、製造、銷售、維修及售
前售後服務，同時為全球第三大滾珠螺桿廠和線性滑軌廠，僅
次於日本 THK、NSK。公司以自有品牌 「HIWIN」 行銷全球，
海外子公司橫跨德國、美國、日本、瑞士、捷克、法國，

⚫ 漲價效益浮現+UTR 提升，26Q2 毛利率攀升至 34.17% 
上銀 26Q2 單季 EPS 攀升至 2.40 元。自 5、6 月起，產品
漲價效益逐漸顯現，加上亞洲市場需求延續帶動交期延長；其
中 ， 機器人產品受惠於半導體需求強勁 ， 出貨量年增達 7 成。
第二季合併營收達 75.08 億元 ，QoQ 17.70%，YoY 26.71%。 
獲利表現方面，受惠於漲價效益與 UTR 提升，單季毛利率達

券餘(張) 156 57 34.17%，季增 2.22 個百分點，年增 4.43 個百分點。在營

⚫ 預估營收獲利季增，帶動 26Q3 單季 EPS 達 2.81 元 
上銀 8 月營收 27.08 億元 ，MoM 0.98%，YoY 42.30%；1-8
月累計營收 192.77 億元，YoY 23.03% 。研究部預期，9 月
營收有望延續雙位數年增態勢，預估  26Q3 營收將達 80.92 
億元，QoQ 7.77%，YoY 34.97%。 
獲利表現方面，考量第三季將完整反映產品漲價效益，且營收
規 模 擴 張 帶 動  UTR 提 升 ， 預 估  26Q3 毛 利 率 將 達 
35.43%，季增 1.27 個百分點，年增 6.83 個百分點。預估
單季營業利益達 12.00 億元 ，QoQ 16.11%，YoY 192.40%；
稅後淨利 9.94 億元，QoQ 17.25% ，YoY 122.90% ，單季 
EPS 預估為 2.81 元。

⚫ 全球需求延續，獲利有望持續成長，估 2026 年 EPS 9.28 元 
研究部對上銀後市營收維持正向看待，係因以下：(1) 終端需
求持續暢旺，產品交期維持高位； (2) 公司具備彈性定價能
力，漲價效益足以覆蓋成本上揚；(3) 半導體產業需求穩固，
持續帶動滾珠螺桿與機器人等相關產品出貨。 
訂單能見度方面，目前整體產品交期維持高檔，線性滑軌約  
4-5 個月 ， 滾珠螺桿最長可達 6 個月 。 觀察各區域下游需求：
亞洲市場維持暢旺，其中中國受惠於電動車、電子與半導體產
業持續擴張，帶動製造設備與傳動元件需求；東南亞受惠全球
供應鏈重組，除傳統傳動元件外， 亦間接推升機器人產品需
求；台灣則持續受惠半導體與科技業資本支出，帶動螺桿與機
器人穩健成長。歐洲市場方面，儘管德國汽車工業仍顯疲態，
但軍工與航太產業已成為新成長動能，加上捷克、波蘭等中歐
製造核心需求保持穩定，有效支撐歐洲營運。

生產與成本端方面，雖地緣政治因素推升包材、潤滑油、銑刀
及鋼材等原物料價格 ， 但目前產品的漲價幅度已足以抵銷成本
波動，加上公司針對鋼材維持多元採購策略以對衝風險，研究
部預估上銀 2026 至 2027 年毛利率與營業利益將呈穩健成
長態勢。 
綜上所述，隨需求延續，研究部預估上銀 2026/2027 年營收
為 296.90 億 / 329.36 億，YoY 22.37% / 10.93%；伴隨營運
規模提升帶動獲利表現 ， 毛利率預估為 34.08% / 34.39%，年
增 5.32 / 0.31 個百分點；營業利益預估為 38.85 億 / 50.93
億，YoY 136.44% / 31.09%；EPS 預估為 9.28 / 11.87 元。

⚫ 結論與投資建議：
上銀 26Q2 受惠於產品漲價效益顯現及 UTR 提升，單季毛利
率 攀 升 至 34.17% 。 單 季 合 併 營 收 達75.08 億元， QoQ
17.70%、YoY 26.71%。營業利益與稅後淨利分別大幅成長至
10.34 億元與 8.48 億元，帶動單季 EPS 攀升至 2.40 元。
展望後市，研究部對上銀營收表現維持正向看待。目前終端需
求持續暢旺，產品交期維持高位，線性滑軌能見度約 4 至 5
個月，滾珠螺桿最長可達 6 個月。儘管地緣政治因素推升鋼
材、包材與潤滑油等原物料價格，但公司具備彈性定價能力且
維持多元採購策略，漲價效益足以抵銷成本上揚。伴隨 26Q3
完整反映漲價產品銷售，預估單季營收達 80.92 億元，QoQ

7.77%，YoY 34.97%，EPS 上看 2.81 元。
放眼 2026 至 2027 年，亞洲半導體需求穩固、歐洲軍工與航
太產業接棒成為新動能，整體訂單需求無虞。預估上銀 2026
年與 2027 年營收將分別達 296.90 億元與 329.36 億元 ， 維持
雙位數年增幅，YoY 分別為 22.37%與 10.93%。在營運規模
提升帶動下，預估 2026 年、2027 年 EPS 將分別達 9.28 元
與 11.87 元。
評價方面 ， 上銀過去五年PER 主要集中於 20 倍至 40 倍區間 。
考量公司營收與獲利將呈現雙位數成長上升趨勢 ， 產業結構持
續上行，研究部以2027 年預估 EPS 11.87 元作為評價基礎，
並給予歷史區間中緣偏上之 35x 本益比，目標價為 420 元，
給予「買進」之投資建議。

股價相對大盤走勢

聯電 (2303 TT)  Buy

中長期業績展望佳，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：展望 2026 年， AI 基礎設施的需求正帶動整體市場
復甦，並外溢至記憶體、網通與電源管理領域，然消費性電子
(如手機、 PC) 終端需求仍偏弱，且庫存天數有微幅上升的跡象。
儘管如此，聯電預期其 2026 年整體晶圓出貨量仍將成長，特別
是在 22/28 nm 與 8 吋的市占率持續提升。聯電預估 2026 年 AI
相關營收將近 3 億美元，並預期 3 年內突破 10 億美元大關。預
估聯電 2026 年稅後 EPS 7.72 元。公司在先進封裝 (interposer 或
矽電容等 )、矽光子及 Intel 合作的 12nm 陸續取得進展，為 2027
年後中長期成長奠定基礎。雖聯電 2Q26 獲利大多以投資收益
為主， 3Q26 股市成長動能相對弱，可能壓抑業外收益，但本業
獲利逐步回升，建議 Buy ，目標價 169 元。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.36 元：聯電 2Q26 營收為 687.33 億元，
QoQ+12.61% ，整體出貨量約當  12 吋晶圓 112.90 萬片，
QoQ+10.58% 。 2Q26 產能利用率增加至  85% 。 ASP 穩定向上。
2Q26 產能利用率增至 85% ， ASP QoQ 小增。 2Q26 毛利率增加
3.28 個百分點至 32.48% 。業外認列  302.36 億元收益，包含利
息收益  2.18 億元、匯兌收益 0.03 億元，認列投資收益  300.45 
億元 (1Q26 則為  49.97 億元投資收益 )。聯電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 
3.36 元 。 若不計算投資收益 ，2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.97 元，QoQ+9.31% 。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元：預期 3Q26 在電腦、通訊及消費
性領域的需求動能將保持穩定。出貨成長主要由電源管理  IC 、
感測器及微控制器（ MCU ）的強勁需求所推動。 8 吋晶圓產品
線正迎來強勁反彈，預期 3Q26 產能利用率將有顯著改善；而  
12 吋產能目前已處於健康的利用率水位，足以支撐核心業務發
展。預 估 聯 電3Q26 晶圓出貨量 QoQ+7~9% ， 產能利用率約 90%。
ASP in USD QoQ+0% ，不過 2026 年的定價環境更具優勢。這主
要源於市場供需關係的結構性改善，毛利率增至約 35% 左右。
預估聯電 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元。

2 群益投顧 
  聯電 ESG ：  
聯電在 ESG 方面持續展現積極作為，並獲國內外多項評鑑肯定：  
永續策略與治理： ESG 由董事會與二位共同總經理組成的指導委員會全盤
規劃，持續以 「以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮」 為願景推動永續。 ESG
績效結合員工分紅與限制型股票條件，提升全員參與動能與企業凝聚力。  
環境面（ E）  
2025 年獲國際永續評鑑（如 FTSE Russell ）評為全台 ESG 表現頂尖企業，
半導體業唯一入選前 3。  
持續推動用水回收、能源效率提升，製程用水回收率在各廠均高於法規標
準。  
積極採購並投資離岸風電，與 CIP 渢妙風場簽 30 年綠電採購合約，展現對
2050 淨零承諾。  
社會面（ S）  
深化產學合作，與台灣 4 所大學半導體專班合作， 600 多位學生參與。  
啟動員工持股信託，落實多元薪酬政策，提升員工福祉並吸引人才。  
長期投入社會公益，如偏鄉關懷、青年培力、綠色創新等持續進行。  
公司治理（ G）  
董事會組成高度多元且獨立，獨董占三分之二、女性董事占三分之一。  
強化利害關係人溝通與資訊透明度，持續強化供應鏈 ESG 要求，並納入評
核與管理系統，推動價值鏈共榮。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

 AI 不泡沫 功率就有夢

   公司簡介 
強茂為台灣功率半導體與分離式元件製造大廠，公司採垂直整合佈局，擁有 3 座前段
晶圓製造廠，及 5 座海內外後段封裝測試據點。

買進 208 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，150 
【大盤指數/股價】45849 / 168.5

➢ 2Q26 獲利符合預期 
強茂 2Q26 營收 39.56 億元，QoQ+15.62%，YoY+16.34%，毛利率
33.22%， 稅後淨利4.88 億元 ，QoQ+69.58%，YoY+93.85%，EPS 1.28
元，獲利大致符合預期。毛利率維持季增趨勢，但營業費用比預期高，
主因是客戶拉貨強勁，備貨及物流相關費用多及提列獎金所致。 
➢ 下半年營收、獲利逐季向上 
AI 伺服器、車用及小訊號產品訂單能見度佳，強茂 B/B ratio 值維持在
1.3~1.5 高檔位置，3Q26 新增部份產能，加上產品調漲，預期營收及毛
利率持續向上；4Q26 有機會切入另一家 CSP 供應鏈，年底增加封測產
能(小訊號為主)、越南 TOREX 預計在 1Q27 交割，整體而言營運動能無
虞。預估強茂 3Q26 營收 42.51 億元，QoQ+7.47%，YoY+30.02%，
毛利率 34.44%，稅後淨利 5.58 億元，QoQ+14.39%，YoY+84.08%，
EPS 1.46 元。 
➢ 歐美重心轉移、非紅供應鏈，台 IDM 廠有訂價權 
在 AI 需求強勁推動下，單機櫃功耗持續上升並導入高壓直流電架構，帶
動功率元件量價同步提升，應用於伺服器功率元件年複合成長率遠高於
產業平均，在產能吃緊情況下，歐美 IDM 廠將重心轉向利潤較佳的高
壓、SiC 以及低壓大電流的 DrMOS 核心市場，排擠中低壓功率元件產
能。同時，安世、揚傑等中資背景企業面臨去中化，伺服器、車用工控
等領域加速將訂單轉向台廠。 
➢ 預估強茂 2027 年 EPS 6.95 元，建議買進，目標價 208 元 
投研部預估強茂 2026 年營收 159.39 億元，YoY+21.73%，稅後淨利
19.21 億元，YoY+61.22%，EPS 5.03 元；預估 2027 年營收 183.38 億
元，YoY+15.05%，稅後淨利 26.57 億元，YoY+38.29%，EPS 6.95 元。
目前強茂 PE 24X ，2026 年最高本益比已超越 2017~2018 年、
2020~2022 年功率元件景氣循環 20X~30X 區間，AI 伺服器的應用有
助拉升功率元件產業及公司評價，給予強茂買進建議，目標價 208 元
(2027F PE 30X)。

2481 強茂 加權指數

個股報告

公司簡介 
強茂股份有限公司成立於 1986 年，為台灣功率半導體與分離式元件製造大廠，公司
採垂直整合(IDM)佈局，擁有 3 座前段晶圓製造廠(4 吋、6 吋、8 吋各一座)，及 5 座
海內外後段封裝測試據點。 
1H26 產品營收比重 ： 金氧半場效電晶體(MOSFET)佔 32%， 蕭特基二極體(Schottky)
佔 23%，整流器(Rectifier)佔 12%，突波抑制器(TVS)佔 9%，靜電防護二極體(ESD 
Diode)佔 7%，齊納二極體(Zener)佔 6%，開關二極體(Switching)佔 6%，雙極性接
面電晶體(BJT)佔 4%，碳化矽(SiC)佔 1%。若以市場應用來看，車用電子佔營收比重
35%為最大，消費型電子佔 20%，電腦運算佔 16%，工控&綠能佔 13%，電源供應
用佔 12%，通訊佔 4%，其中 AI 人工智能應用佔 11%。

淨利 4.88 億元，QoQ+69.58%，YoY+93.85%，EPS 1.28 元，獲利大致符合預期。
毛利率維持季增趨勢，但營業費用比預期高，主因是客戶拉貨強勁，備貨及物流相關
費用多及提列將金所致。

個股報告 
下半年營收、獲利逐季向上 
AI 伺服器、車用及小訊號產品訂單能見度佳，強茂 B/B ratio 值維持在 1.3~1.5 高檔
位置，強茂 8 月營收 13.76 億元，MoM-4.89%，YoY+29.82%，3Q26 新增部份產
能，加上產品調漲，預期營收及毛利率持續向上，4Q26 有機會切入另一家 CSP 供應
鏈，年底增加封測產能(小訊號為主)、越南 TOREX 預計在 1Q27 交割，整體而言營運
動能無虞。預估強茂 3Q26 營收 42.51 億元，QoQ+7.47%，YoY+30.02%，毛利率
34.44%，稅後淨利 5.58 億元，QoQ+14.39%，YoY+84.08%，EPS 1.46 元。 
歐美重心轉移、非紅供應鏈，台 IDM 廠有訂價權 
在 AI 需求強勁推動下 ， 單機櫃功耗持續上升並導入高壓直流電架構 ， 帶動功率元件量
價同步提升，應用於伺服器功率元件年複合成長率遠高於產業平均，在產能吃緊情況
下，歐美 IDM 廠將重心轉向利潤較佳的高壓、SiC 以及低壓大電流的 DrMOS 核心市
場，排擠中低壓功率元件產能。同時，安世、揚傑等中資背景企業面臨去中化，伺服
器、車用工控等領域加速將訂單轉向台廠。 
目前成熟製程與後段封裝產能極度吃緊，具備晶圓代工、晶片設計到封裝完整布局的 
強茂等 IDM 廠，憑藉產品線的廣度及垂直整合優勢，能確保供貨不中斷並免受外包
產能排擠 ， 更實質掌握了稀缺產能的訂價權 ， 在這一波由AI 架構升級與地緣政治雙軌
驅動的轉單潮中，將成為優先且最大受惠者。 
機櫃功率愈大，功率元件量價同步提升 
AI 應用佔強茂營收比重 11%，多數來自運算托盤(compute tray)上 Power MOSFET、
Schottky、TVS 或小訊號等功率產品，運算托盤上功率元件產值佔整機櫃超過一半，
主要核心在整合式供電模組(DrMOS)，DrMOS 技術門檻高，由歐美大廠主導，台廠
不易切入，但供電相數的增加，將同步提升對周邊功率元件的需求量。 
以 GB300 為例，單顆 GPU 功率 1.4kW，電壓 0.8V，核心負載輸出電流是 1,750A，
根據單顆高密度 DrMOS / SPS 晶片的最大承載電流(70A~130A)規格去設計供電相
數(Power Phase) ，每一相數都必須配置一套完整的硬體元件，當 GPU 功率提升至
2.3kW (Vera Rubin) ，GPU 功率增加 60%，供電相數及功率元件需求量亦將等幅放
大。 強茂功率元件已成功打入CSP 大廠 ， 未來將持續受惠運算托盤上功率元件量的提
升及質的改變(如 TVS 相對大電流，晶粒面積變大，單價提升)，產業成長趨勢明確。 
預估強茂 2027 年 EPS 6.95 元，建議買進，目標價 208 元 
投研部預估強茂 2026 年營收 159.39 億元，YoY+21.73%，稅後淨利 19.21 億元，
YoY+61.22%，EPS 5.03 元；預估 2027 年營收 183.38 億元，YoY+15.05%，稅後淨
利 26.57 億元，YoY+38.29%，EPS 6.95 元。目前強茂 PE 24X，2026 年最高本益比
已超越 2017~2018 年、2020~2022 年功率元件景氣循環 20X~30X 區間，AI 伺服
器的應用有助拉升功率元件產業及公司評價， 給予強茂買進 建議，目標價 208 元
(2027F PE 30X)。

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關
條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
1 
2606 裕民航運 ( U-MING )

報告日期： 2026 / 09 / 10 研 究 員：林奕頎（ Yichi Lin ） 
壹、投 資 建 議 
一.預期  202 6 年 稅後 EPS + 8.22 元， YoY + 91%，創  18 年新高  
2024 年全年  BDI 平均  1,755  ； 2025 年全年  BDI 平均  1,681 。 
2026 年（截至 9 月 8 日）  BDI 平均  2,5 12： 
1Q / 2026 單季 BDI 平均  1,955； 2Q / 2026 單季 BDI 平均  2,751。 
3Q / 2026 單季（截至 9 月 8 日） BDI 平均  2,9 28。 
2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日  BDI 3,5 84。 運價 強勢，推升航商獲利 。 
二.美伊衝 突日益激烈 ，荷姆斯海峽封閉，油輪運價快速上漲  
裕民 船隊有  3 艘原油油輪  及 1 艘成品油輪 。  美伊 停戰 破局 ： 
原油油輪 運價指數：從 7 月 2 日 1,850 ，漲至 9 月 8 日 2,938 ，
累計漲幅  59%  ； 成品油輪 運價指數：從 7 月 7 日 995，漲至
9 月 8 日 1,702，累計漲幅  71%。 油輪運價 上漲，裕民將受惠。  
三.預期 4Q 是 2026 年單季獲利高峰 ， 稅後EPS + 2.41 元，YoY + 55%  
(1) 美伊衝突， 荷姆斯海峽 封閉 ，天然氣供給中斷 ； 4Q 取暖
旺季，各國 將被迫 以燃煤取代天然氣，推升海運燃煤需求 。 
(2) 聯合國預期 超級聖嬰現象至少將持續到 2027 年 2 月，極端
氣候橫行 ； 巴拿馬運河缺水 嚴峻，持續降低船舶通行量，
將壓抑海運供給 。  即使超級聖嬰解除；  依過去 5 年資料
（參閱圖 1）， 巴拿馬運河季節性乾季最低水位會落在 5 月。 
(3) 散裝航運以  4Q 為例行的季節性 旺季 。 
基於 (1) ~ (3) ，宏遠預期 4Q 將是裕民  2026 年單季獲利高峰。  
四.基於保守，估 2027 年 BDI 僅 2,490 ，裕民稅後 EPS + 7.10 元 
2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日  BDI 指數  3,5 84。 
宏遠 基於保守 原則，假設  三大利空在 2027 年落實 ： 
(1) 荷姆斯 海峽不再受阻 ，天然氣供給上升，壓抑煤炭需求 。 
(2) 超級 聖嬰解 除，巴拿馬運河不再缺水。  
(3) 全球散裝 船舶 下水量大增 。 
宏遠 估 2027 年 BDI 平均值僅  2,490； 裕民稅後 EPS + 7.10 元。 
若上述三項利空沒有落實，則必須調高裕民  2027 年獲利預估 。 
五.建議： 4Q 旺季  遇上  戰爭及聖嬰；  估 2026 年獲利，18 年新高  
2026 年截至 9 月 8 日，BDI 指數都 大幅 高於 2025 年同期（參閱
圖 2）； 此外，美伊戰爭造成 荷姆斯海峽中斷 ，須以燃煤取代
天然氣，推升散裝海運需求；  而 超級聖嬰 帶來全球乾旱，即
將撞上  巴拿馬運河  每年 12 月 ~ 隔年 5 月的 季節性 乾季（參
閱圖 1），巴拿馬運河缺水將更加嚴重；  宏遠 估裕民 2026 年稅
後 EPS  + 8.22 元，YoY + 91%，創 18 年新高 ； 持續 建議買進 。 
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：101.0 元 
   1.70 X 2027 年底每股淨值

參考價位：79.7 元 
   1.34 X 2027 年底每股淨值 
   2026 / 09 / 09 收盤

BDI 走勢，強於預期 
第三度調升 
裕民 2026 年稅後 EPS 預估值 
從 7.68 元，調升至 8.22 元。 
預期 創 最近 18 年新高

2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日 
BDI  3,5 84，YoY + 78% 
又遇上 
(1) 荷姆斯海峽中斷 
(2) 巴拿馬運河缺水（參閱圖 1） 
預期 4Q 旺季 BDI 漲勢將更強勁

裕民新船下水 遇上 BDI 強勁走
勢，有利於推升獲利 
(1) 2026 年新增 1 艘，運力 + 1%。 
(2) 2027 年新增 5 艘，運力 + 7%。 
(3) 2028 年新增 7 艘，運力 + 9%。

散裝航運主要是出租船舶 
燃油成本由客戶自行負擔 
因此，散裝航商的營運成本 
幾乎不受 燃油漲跌 的 影響

貳、公 司 概 況 
一.公司簡介  
截至 2026 年 5 月（法說資料），裕民實際經營船隻為 71 艘：  
海岬型船   25 艘：自有 22 艘 + 3 艘合資。  
巴拿馬型船 24 艘：自有 14 艘 + 7 艘代管  + 3 艘合資 。 
輕便型      8 艘（自有） 。 
水泥船      4 艘（自有） 。 
油輪        4 艘：透過  環能海運 （持股 40% ）。 
CTV 離岸風電人員運輸船 6 艘（自有  ； 裕民風能航運 ）。

在建船隻  12 艘，交船時程 ： 
202 6 年： 輕便型  1 艘（預期 11 月交付）。 
202 7 年 及 2028 年： 
海岬型  4 艘 + 輕便型  5 艘 + 風電工程船  2 艘。

二.營運概況 及展望  
1.超級聖嬰 造成缺水  撞上  巴拿馬運河乾季，壓抑海運供給  
2026 年 5 月，全球進入聖嬰年，加劇全球乾旱與極端高溫 的
氣候異常 。 聯合國  世界氣象組織（ WMO ）表示， 這波聖嬰
現象，可能是過去  40 年最強一次，幾乎肯定會持續到  2027
年 2 月，很可能打破  2023 年 ~ 2024 年的紀錄 。

巴拿馬運河溝通大西洋與太平洋，節省海運船舶至少  3,000
海哩的航程，航行時間縮短  2 週左右。

但由於 大西洋與太平洋的海平面和水流高度不同，船隻 要透
過 巴 拿 馬 運 河 ，橫 跨 美 洲 ， 必 須依 靠 巴 拿 馬 運 河 的船閘 注
水，採取類似 爬樓梯一樣 的升降 動作 ，讓船 隻從海平面被提
升到高處的加通湖，穿過湖面後，再降回另一側的海平面。
每艘船舶 每次通過船閘，都需要消耗  加通湖  數以百萬加侖
計算的淡水。

因此， 加通湖的水量多寡 ，會影響巴拿馬運河的運作效率與
容量 ，一旦缺水，巴拿馬運河每日通行船舶數量就會減少。

觀察過去 5 年（2021 年 ~ 2025 年）加通湖的水位資料（參閱
圖 1） ，通常會從每年 5 月，逐月爬升到  12 月；  但 2026 年
超級聖嬰的乾旱，造成目前 9 月水位不升反降 。 
以現貨為主，長約為輔 
總船隊 71 艘 
全球 超級聖嬰 極端氣候 
從 2026 年 5 月 至少持續 
至 2027 年 2 月

巴拿美運河 季節性乾季 從 
每年 12 月 持續至 隔年 5 月

聖嬰 + 乾季 
預期巴拿馬運河 要解除缺水 
最快必須等到 2027 年 2H

2026 年 5 月起，全球 超級聖嬰 ，帶來的 乾旱，至少將持續到
2027 年 2 月 ； 如此，將撞上  加通湖  每年  例行 的 季節 性 
乾季 （每年 12 月 ~ 隔年 5 月 ； 參閱圖 1 ）。

因此， 巴拿馬運河（ 加通湖）的缺水很可能會持續加重，最
快的解除時間可能要等到  2027 年 2H。

巴拿馬運河  每日正常通行量  36 ~ 40 艘。 
2023 年 ~ 2024 年 全球 聖嬰 造成乾旱  巴拿馬運河的 每日船
舶通行量最低降至  18 艘。

目前  加通湖  尚未進入  季節性乾季，但 受制於全球聖嬰現
象， 加通湖  水位  已經 提前 明顯降低 ； 巴拿馬運河管理當
局， 已經 宣布，巴拿馬運河每日船舶通行數量， 2026 年 9 月
3 日降至  34 艘 ； 9 月 15 日降至  32 艘。

宏遠預期， 巴拿馬運河降低每日船舶通行數量的趨勢，將日
益嚴重， 壓抑所有的海運供給 ，將有利於推升各型船舶的運
價，拉高海運航商獲利 。

圖 1：加通湖 過去 5 年 每月平均水位變化 ； 5 月 走升至 12 月（雨季） ； 12 月 再走跌至 5 月（乾季） 
超級聖嬰帶來乾旱，造成 2026 年 9 月水位 不升反降 。

單位：英尺。 
資料來源： 網路公開資訊  ； 宏遠  。

2.散裝 BDI 走勢，隱示  2026 年獲利  將大幅優於  2025 年 
2026 年截至 9 月 8 日，散裝 BDI 運價走勢都優於 2025 年同期
表現（ 參閱圖 2） 。 
受惠於 BDI 的強勁走勢 ，裕民 ： 
1Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 1.17 元，優於  1Q / 2025 同期  + 0.27 元。 
2Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 2.34 元，優於  2Q / 2025 同期  + 0.77 元。

從 BDI 的表現來看：  
1Q / 2026 （過年淡季） BDI 平均  1,955 。 
2Q / 2026 （美伊戰爭爆發， 燃煤替代天然氣） BDI 平均  2,751 。 
3Q / 2026 （截至 9 月 8 日） BDI 平均  2,928。 
2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日  BDI  3,584 。

4Q 是散裝 航運的 季節性 旺季，遇上， 荷姆斯海峽封閉  推升  
煤 炭 的 海 運 需 求  及   巴 拿 馬 運 河 缺 水 降低  海運船舶供
給（繞道等同減少供給） ； 預期 4Q 的 BDI 將會持續強勁。

BDI 漲勢 強勁，宏遠預期 裕民  3Q ~ 4Q / 2026 單季獲利 YoY 大
幅成長 ； 未來， 3Q 及 4Q 財報發布 ，將激勵股價表現 。

圖 2：BDI 走勢  與  裕民 單季稅後 EPS 的互動

### Page 3

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
07 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Asia Vital Components
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and the expansion of its addressable market.
Our TP of NT$3,800 is derived from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified given the
company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment, rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing

value of thermal content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural drivers still in place, we expect
the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a High Risk rating is not supported by other
qualitative factors such as cold-plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not been applied.
Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2)

heightened price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling
technologies.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales +20% MoM/+3%
Y oY
In this report, we focus on E Ink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$3,446mn (+20% MoM/+3% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 63% of our 3Q26 revenue estimate of NT$9,985mn (-2%
QoQ/-4% YoY) and 62% of consensus at NT$10,213mn (flat QoQ/-2% YoY).
Our view:
• The solid rebound in the Aug monthly sales should alleviate the potential

risk of missing full-year guidance again (now at 10-15% YoY for 2026), in our
view, as  3Q26 revenue is tracking in line with our estimate for  a modest
decline QoQ.
• While the worst seems to be behind us and valuation is at a trough level of
12x 2027 P/E, we see no strong catalysts in the near term, despite our
positive long-term view on  e-paper demand across various applications.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus

EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 6

Aspeed Technology (5274.TWO)
09 September 2026 Citi Research
6
Aspeed Technology
Company description
Established in 2004 and listed in 2013, Aspeed is a leading IC design
company based in Taiwan focusing on baseboard management controller
(BMC) solutions. Aspeed started the business by targeting datacenter
servers/white-box markets and has been working closely with server ODMs.
Following the acquisition of Emulex Pilot from AVGO/Broadcom in 2016,

Aspeed successfully expanded its product lines, IP pool as well as customer
base and became the largest BMC supplier with c.70% of global market share.
Other than BMC, Aspeed is also broadening its product portfolio by including
bridge IC (BIC; or mini-BMC), platform firmware resilience (PFR) solutions and
input/output (I/O) expander, aiming to maximize the dollar content it could
generate per server motherboard.
Investment strategy
We rate Aspeed shares a Buy. As a leading BMC supplier with c.70%+ global

market share, it has been riding the strong momentum underpinned by a
recovery of general-purpose servers and upticks in AI servers. We expect the
growth to continue. Apart from being in a dominant position for the supply of
upcoming GB200 server BMCs with higher dollar content and better capex
efficiency, new product launches including AST2700, PFR, I/O expander and
Cupola 360, as well as steady growth in general-purpose servers should also
act as key drivers for the company.

Valuation
We value Aspeed at NT$25,000 based on a historical mid-cycle multiple
(since 2020) of 50x 2028E EPS, as we believe it fairly reflects the company’s
earnings performance and industry dynamics, secular growth trends, and
Aspeed’s strong supply chain position, as well as robust earnings growth
amid margin expansions.
Risks
Our quant rating system rates the Aspeed stock High Risk based on its
volatility over the past year, which we think is unwarranted given the

company's dominance and outlook in the BMC sector. Key downside risks to
our earnings forecasts and target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected
growth in servers; 2) slower-than-expected CPU platform migration; 3) peer
competition; and 4) limited progress in product diversification.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

iPhone 18: Real Upgrade Cycle,

estimates.
We expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to be the key
beneficiaries of the new iPhone launch. We downgrade Largan
to EW and Genius to UW.  Both can benefit from the variable
aperture lens upgrade, but enthusiasm over Co-Packaged Optics

has made the stocks overvalued.
The iPhone opportunity is real for Largan and Genius…but their stocks have
rallied 48% and 85%, respectively, in the past month: (The TAIEX is up 5% over
this period.)
We now value Largan at NT$7,100/share – no longer attractive: Our analysis
suggests that Largan's current share price already embeds more than 30% global
market share in Fiber Array Units (FAU) before formal production starts in 2H27 or
even 2028. Our residual income model yields a value of NT$3,000/share for the

company's traditional business, while our TAM analysis derives a value of NT$4,100/
share for CPO/FAU new business, bringing the total price target to NT$7,100.
However, any delay in CPO/FAU development poses significant downside risk. We
therefore downgrade to EW.
We now value Genius at NT$910/share – but  even on this basis, we view it as
overvalued: Similarly, we believe Genius's share price is also inflated by hopes for
FAU components. Using a similar approach, we conclude that aggregate value is NT

$910/share (NT$600 for existing business and NT$310/share for FAU component
business). We recommend taking profits.
Reiterate OW on Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal and AAC: We believe all four companies
will benefit from the new iPhone launch. Here, however,  we still see upside in the
stocks. We see these companies, benefiting in various ways:
• Luxshare: Key player in iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max assembly, also benefits from
variable aperture upgrade as component supplier.

•  Lingyi: Higher content value form both iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and foldable.
•  Crystal: Micro prism modules, coated filters, and cover lenses should enjoy
positive ASP and volume growth.
• AAC: Vapor chamber (VC) heat dissipation upgrade for iPhone 18 Pro Max
and Pro is the key catalyst.
Remain EW on Sunny Optical and GoerTek: Sunny is a key variable aperture
component vendor, but it's been well on track to meet the goal of doubling revenue,
implying limited upside surprise. GoerTek could face labor shortages and margin

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Executive Summary
With Largan and Genius's significant outperformance YTD, we believe the iPhone 18
launch is less likely to become a key driver of further stock price movement for them.
Instead, our analysis suggests that the CPO enthusiasm could have already made these
stocks overvalued. We therefore downgrade Largan from OW to  EW and downgrade
Genius from EW  to UW.
Meanwhile, we expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to become the key beneficiaries

amid iPhone new product launches. These stocks haven't rallied materially, and we see
upside potential in them.
We maintain EW ratings on Sunny and GoerTek and expect limited positive surprise from
the iPhone peak season.

### Page 5

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Implications from iPhone 18 Launch for Various Supply
Chain Companies
Camera spec upgrade is a positive earnings driver, but CPO
enthusiasm has made Largan and Genius overvalued
The iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max have applied a variable aperture main camera for the first
time, representing a significant hardware upgrade.  The ASP of  variable aperture lenses will
be higher than it is for traditional lenses. Thus, a rapid shipment increase in 3Q26 could

become a key re-rating catalyst – indeed, it has already prompted sharp re-rating for
Largan, Genius, and Sunny.
Besides this, the launch of Apple's first foldable iPhone will also provide an incremental
demand driver beyond the standard iPhone 18 lineup. According to Nikkei Asia (link),
foldable smartphone orders increased from 8 million units to 10 million units this year,
and if the new product performs well in 4Q2026, it could become a positive catalyst for

Apple supply chain companies.
Beyond variable aperture and foldable, the further development of a new periscope lens
with the key customer is likely to expand  these use of periscope lenses in the future. This
could also become a positive driver for optical lens companies in the supply chain.
1. Largan: iPhone opportunity is real, but recent enthusiasm
over CPO made valuation too aggressive – downgrade to EW
Largan's revenue follows a well-established seasonal pattern tied to Apple's annual iPhone

launch cycle. After shipments declined continuously from March 2026 through June 2026,
we expect a seasonal recovery to begin in 3Q26, with sequential increases highly likely in
the following months. Largan's August 2026 consolidated revenue reached NT$5.012
billion, up 3% month-over-month and its best performance in four months, and the
company expects September's shipment momentum to surpass August's.
Largan is a key supplier of variable aperture lenses for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and the

company could also benefit from foldable and periscope opportunities, we raise our
intrinsic value estimate of the company's traditional business from NT$2,814/share to NT
$3,000/share. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 18.
Besides the Apple lens opportunity, we believe Largan's share performance is also highly
correlated to CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO
opportunity, we undertook the following analysis:

1. We forecast CPO optical engine volume to increase from 0.39mn in 2026 to
7.78mn in 2027,with a further surge to 48.6mn in 2028. Consequently, FAU (Fiber
Array Units – vital optical interconnect components that align multiple optical
fibers with silicon chips, making them critical for Co-Packaged Optics) volume for
CPO also grows from 0.39 to 48.6 mn for the same period of time;
2. We assume ASP of US$100 in 2026-2028 (average market level);
3. We forecast Largan's FAU market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
2027 and 40% in 2028 (previously 20%).
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 30% in 2028, we forecast FAU earnings
of US$4mn in 2027 and US$579 mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU business at 35x 2028e P/E (industry peers are at 40-50x P/E, and
considering the first year of scalable production), it could bring incremental market cap of
US$20.278bn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% rate, we derive a per-share positive
impact of NT$4,100.

We hold a positive view on Largan's CPO/FAU business opportunity because the company
has a superior track record of high precision manufacturing relative to most industry peers
in Taiwan. However, our calculation above suggests that the current share price already
reflects Largan's potential  to attain >30% global market share for FAU products.
Meanwhile, formal production hasn't kicked off yet – it is more likely to start in the

second half of 2027 or even 2028. Therefore, any delay in CPO/FAU development could
result in significant share price fluctuation. Considering current risk-reward, even after
raising our price target 47%, we no longer view Largan as attractive any more in view of
the recent strong share price performance. We set our price target at NT$7,100, within
which NT$3,000 is from its traditional business and NT$4,100 is from CPO/FAU new
business.
2. Genius: Thesis is similar to Largan's –  CPO opportunities

also overpriced, downgrade to UW
Genius, as the second-largest optical lens vendor to Apple, is also well positioned to
benefit from the camera spec upgrade highlighted in the previous section. Considering the
growth opportunities discussed above, we raise our intrinsic value estimate for the

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
existing business from NT$425 to NT$600. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 17.
Similar to Largan, we believe Genius' share price performance is also highly correlated to
CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO opportunity,
we undertook the following analysis:
1. We apply the same FAU volume and ASP assumptions as we do for Largan.
2. We expect Genius to be involved in FAU components, rather than overall

solutions; therefore, we assume components TAM to be 15% of the FAU TAM.
3. We forecast Genius's market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in 2027 and
30% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 25% in 2028, we forecast FAU
component earnings of US$1mn in 2027 and US$54mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU component business at 25x 2028e P/E (lower multiple vs. Largan
because Genius has smaller scale), it could introduce incremental market cap of US

$1,322mn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% discounted rate, we derive a per-share
positive impact of NT$310.

Similar to Largan, our residual income model derives intrinsic value of NT$600/share for
the traditional business, while the TAM analysis suggests CPO/FAU-related opportunities
could be valued at NT$310/share. Adding these together, we raise our price target

materially to NT$910, but that still implies 14% downside from the latest closing price.
3. Sunny: Apple revenue to double in 2026, but positive is
well known
We believe Sunny Optical will benefit from the new iPhone launch because:
1. The company is a key vendor of  variable aperture actuators – a key spec upgrade

### Page 8

M
Idea
8
with higher ASP.
2. Sunny is expanding the product mix from a single category to multiple categories,
which could generate higher revenue from the key client.
3. Sunny is also gaining share in high-end lens products.
These drivers collectively underpin management's guidance of doubling  Apple revenue for
the full-year of 2026.
However, the fundamental performance is in line with management's guidance, so there is
lack of potential positive surprise, in our view. We therefore raise our net income

estimates 29.6% for 2026 and 26.5% for 2027 based on upbeat 1H26 results.
Consequently, we raise our price target to HK$75 but maintain our EW rating –  the strong
growth from Apple has been well expected.
4. Crystal: Prisms, coated filters, and cover lenses to benefit
We expect Crystal Optech to benefit from Apple's new product launch because  the
company is supplying three key products in the supply chain:
1. Its micro prism modules are a key enabling component for periscope (folded-

optics) telephoto cameras in smartphones. The company's prism technology uses a
folded optical path to achieve long-focal-length telephoto capability without
increasing device thickness. In 1H26, the micro prism module business enjoyed
significant volume growth. The iPhone 18 launch will directly extend this demand
into 2H26.
2. Crystal-Optech's coated filter business — which brings micro-nano optical
processing into the IRCF manufacturing process to significantly improve petal

ghosting and chromatic aberration in special imaging scenarios — is another key
segment that benefits from the iPhone 18 launch. Apple's high-end Pro models are
the primary application for this product, so the company is highly likely to
generate rapid revenue and earnings growth.
3. The iPhone 18 Pro models are expected to feature advances in 3D sensing,
including a smaller Dynamic Island as some Face ID components move beneath the
display, which sustains demand for these optical components. Crystal Optech is

well positioned to capture the growth opportunity in iPhone's 3D sensing and
cover lenses.
The company's 2Q26 results were a bit weaker than we expected because of rising costs
from R&D and capacity expansion related to new products. However, we believe this
investment could lead to stronger growth in 2028 and onward. As a result, we trim our
earnings estimates 15.6% for 2026 and 2.9% for 2027 but raise our 2028 estimate 8.2%.
Consequently, we raise our price target  7%, to Rmb32, implying 24% upside from the

current level.
Luxshare: Apple opportunity underappreciated,  resilient
growth intact
We believe Luxshare's Apple opportunity is underappreciated –  some investors worry
about the supply chain relocation. However, the company is one of Apple's most deeply

### Page 9

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 9
embedded manufacturing partners, covering various products and components, as
illustrated in the chart below. The company has  also proved its operational resilience,
consistently registering positive growth in the Apple business.
Exhibit 7: Luxshare: One of the most deeply embedded manufacturing partners for Apple
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
We expect Luxshare to benefit from the latest new product  launch through the following

four key drivers:
1.  Second largest assembler for iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max — the most premium-heavy
iPhone lineup in history. If the  Pro/Pro Max models can take 70%+ of total iPhone
volume, it could mark the highest high-end model contribution over the past
several model cycles. A higher share from high-end models implies better revenue
and margin outlook for Luxshare.
2.   Capturing component opportunity in new  variable aperture actuator supply.

Instead of doing assembly, Lxushare is also playing the role  of supplier of actuator
mechanisms for the variable aperture camera system in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro
Max. This helps Luxshare gain exposure to high-end component business, which
carries higher margin than assembly business.
3. Foldable iPhone (iPhone Ultra) exposure: While Luxshare is not the assembler for
the foldable phone, it has actively developed foldable hinge technology, with R&D

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
focused on high-precision, long-life, large-angle hinge modules for inward-folding
flexible-display devices. The company is also developing ultra-thin haptic motors
tailored for foldable smartphones. When foldable shipments reach a certain scale,
Luxshare is also highly likely to become the second assembler.
4.  Leading AirPods assembly share:  In the AirPods assembly market, we expect
Luxshare to remain the largest supplier, with GoerTek as the second player. The

iPhone 18 launch is accompanied by new AirPods models, and Luxshare's dominant
AirPods assembly position means that it captures the full breadth of Apple's 2H26
product cycle — not just the iPhone.
Despite a significant FX loss, Luxshare posted resilient earnings in 1H26. We nudge our
EPS estimates up 0.2% each for 2026 and 2027 and 2.0% for 2028 to reflect the solid
1H26 results and positive growth outlook in the following 2-3 years. As a result, we also

raise our H-share price target to HK$82/share, implying 34% upside potential.
Lingyi: Peak season to drive strong earnings improvement
Lingyi  provides a broad range of components, including core precision parts, hinge
precision structural components, screen support plates, flanged ultra-thin stainless steel
battery case modules, etc. Following the launch of iPhone 18 Pro, Pro Max, and Apple's
first-ever foldable iPhone, Lingyi is likely to benefit from  higher content per unit on

standard Pro models, and a step-change in content value from the foldable iPhone.
Exhibit 8: Lingyi: Higher content value from new iPhone
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
Lingyi also has good exposure to the iPhone  foldable product, with content value per
foldable device expected by the company to reach US$80–90, including titanium inner
hinges, hinge components, and mid-frame support structures — substantially above the
content value of conventional smartphones.

Considering the positive drivers in 3Q26, we expect Lingyi to achieve significant earnings
improvement on a QoQ basis, which could become a key re-rating catalyst.
We slightly adjust our net income  estimates after 1H26: up 1.6%  in 2026, 0.3% and 1.7% in
2027 and 2028. However, our price targets for both the H- and A-shares are unchanged
because the revision is minor while our long-term RI model assumptions remain

### Page 11

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 11
unchanged.
AAC: Key beneficiary of  iPhone VC application
AAC Tech is one of Apple's key component suppliers, providing a broad portfolio spanning
acoustics, haptics, and thermal management. The iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and iPhone Ultra
launch is a direct, multi-dimensional catalyst across all those business segments.
Within the three segments, we believe thermal management –  Vapor Chamber (VC) Heat
Dissipation – is the most important catalyst. AAC Technologies is a key vendor of VC heat

spreaders in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max. The significant expansion of the VC cooling
area is driven by the iPhone 18 Pro's A20 Pro chip, which debuts on a 2nm process node
with complex WMCM packaging technology to support on-device AI computation, leading
to increased thermal output. Apple first introduced VC liquid cooling in the iPhone 17 Pro
series, with AAC as the primary supplier for that generation as well.
This division has already demonstrated significant growth momentum heading into the

iPhone 18 ramp up. In 1H26, heat dissipation revenue surged approximately 400% YoY, to
RMB1.10 billion, primarily driven by the continued ramp-up in shipments of high-
performance innovative vapor chambers. The company expects VC shipments to exceed
180,000k units in 2026, up from 140,000k+  units in 2025.

Combining the strong growth momentum in thermal management, but weaker-than-
expected performance in the optics segment (all supplied to Android), we raise our
earnings estimates  8.4% for 2026 and 6.0% for 2027. After 1H26 results, we believe
investors are aware of the weakness in optics, while the iPhone launch could help re-rate

the stock. We raise our price target to HK$50 – the stronger 19% increase reflects
earnings upgrades and a higher valuation for the thermal-management business – and
maintain our OW rating.
BYDE: Normal peak season, but lackluster with limited spec
upgrade

### Page 12

M
Idea
12
While Apple is BYDE's key customer, accounting for >50% of total revenue, it is mainly
from the  iPad assembly business. Therefore, this division is less likely to benefit from the
iPhone 18 launch.
The company is also the second-largest iPhone metal casing vendor, supplying precision
structural components including titanium and aluminium alloy chassis. While iPhone 18
launch is the direct driver for this segment, the titanium-to-aluminium downgrade has

become a near-term headwind.  Titanium is a higher-value, higher-margin material for BYDE
while aluminium carries lower ASP and lower gross margin. This change has made BYDE
less likely to benefit from the iPhone 18 launch.
Despite the lackluster outlook, we expect earnings to show sequential improvement in
3Q26, driven by the normal peak season in 3Q26, followed by stronger growth in 4Q26 as
liquid cooling products for AI infrastructure also ramp up. Our earnings estimates and

price target remain unchanged.
GoerTek: Margin might face pressures despite peak season
GoerTek's primary Apple exposure is in smart acoustic complete units (AirPods assembly
and components) and precision components. We believe the  iPhone 18 launch and peak
season ramp in 3Q–4Q will drive higher shipment volumes for these products.
However, GoerTek's transfer of production  to Vietnam — where it has invested
approximately US$1.3 billion through multiple entities — has created a persistent labor

cost headwind. In 2H25, a labor shortage caused by the rapid capacity transfer forced
GoerTek to offer higher wages, triggering material  gross margin pressure  for the smart
acoustic assembly business. Overall gross margins partially recovered to 13.8% in 1Q26
and 15.2% in 2Q26, but the underlying trend of rising wages in Vietnam remains a
structural factor. As GoerTek ramps production for the iPhone 18 peak season in 3Q and
4Q, the higher labor cost base in Vietnam will continue to weigh on assembly margins.

We raise our 2026 and 2027 earnings estimates 14.9% and 10.6%, respectively, mainly
reflecting the better-than-expected gross margin recovery in 1H26. However, we raise our
price target only modestly: 4%. to Rmb27, as persistent labor-cost pressure in Vietnam
could limit further margin expansion and reduce the earnings benefit from higher peak-
season volumes.
Considering the factors discussed above, we believe GoerTek is less likely to benefit from

the new iPhone product launch and peak season volume increase.

Where we could be wrong
While we expect iPhone 18 to be  a positive catalyst in 2H26, we are watching three key
variables on the potential inflection points:
1.  Memory prices: These are a key variable this year. If there is a memory price
correction anytime during 2026, investors are likely to expect an earlier
smartphone shipment and margin recovery, which could reverse the negative

sentiment.
2.  LLMs: We see LLMs as a revolutionary innovation. They could introduce numerous
creative features in smartphone applications and change user habits in the years to
come. If the Edge AI breakthrough arrives earlier than we expect, it could
accelerate the smartphone product cycle and make the recovery earlier.
3. iPhone sales performance: Better-than-expected sell-through of the iPhone 18
Pro, Pro Max and its first foldable model could drive shipment upside, higher

content value and stronger capacity utilization. Conversely, weaker demand could
lead to order cuts after the initial ramp up and reduce the earnings benefits from
key hardware upgrades.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Document 095: 260914_gs_optical-tour

Source PDF: all report pdf/260914_gs_optical-tour.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-14
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

1. Optical module demand outlook: Companies we visited remain positive on 
optical module demand, driven by AI server racks ramp up, optical module adoption 
expansion (from scale out to scale up / across), and specification upgrade. On global 
market demand on 800G and 1.6T, demand could be at 130m to 200m; for the China 
market, overall demand could also grow at mid double digits to 50m+ units, with the 
majority 800G and 400G, and 1.6T emerging. Nevertheless, companies we visited 
with also mentioned that tight raw material supply (e.g. DSP , lasers, PCB) could 
continue to cap shipments in 2027. The positive tone echoes our positive view on 
global demand, on which we raised 800G and above optical module demand by 39% 
/ 36% in 2027E / 2028E to 144m / 171m (report link). Supply chain positive tone 
reflects further upside to our estimates. 
2. Optical module competition: Companies we visited see pricing competition 
remaining healthy, with 800G optical module pricing decline within single digits in 
2027, and 1.6T optical module price to sustain at US$700+. Managements highlight 
that optical module is not a pure assembly but with multiple SKUs and require 
suppliers’ strong R&D and timely development as compared to telecom, the product 
cycle could shorten to 1-2 years in datacom vs. 5 years in telecom. Supply chain 
management and mass production capability are also important to win orders, 
especially under the current tight supply situation and clients’ requirements of 
diversifying production sites under geopolitical tension. Innolight, as the global 
leader works with supply chain closely, including R&D collaboration, invests directly 
in their suppliers, and pre-payment and long-term agreements to secure a

### Page 2

sustainable supply through cycles.
3. Laser supply: Chinese suppliers are ramping up capacity and capability in 200G EML,
supporting the 1.6T optical module ramp up in 2027. Nevertheless, shipment also
depends on other raw material supply (e.g. DSP), and customers penetration.
Everbright‘s laser capacity prepared for 2027 is EML slightly higher than CW laser, and
within the EML majority is for 100G, with the remaining for 200G; Dongshan Precision

highlights their 200G EML is already in mass production, while volume is still small
currently given the tight supply of DSP; nevertheless, the company also secured 10m
supply of DSP in 2027, supporting their lasers and optical modules shipment ramp up.
VPEC, global leading InP epiwafer supplier, also sees improving InP substrate supply, with
steady export permission from China government, more customers bringing InP
substrate, and growing second to fourth suppliers outside of China. VPEC’s MOCVD is

also in expansion, from current 62 MOCVD to 69 MOCVD by 2Q27, and planning a new
production site in 2027.
4. CPO timeline: As a new technology, CPO will naturally take time and could face
challenges in the path of commercialization. For example, laser suppliers see thermal
challenges for 300mW / 400mW CW lasers in ELS (external laser source), and is working
on 100mW+ with PA for NPO first. Optical module suppliers also see strong 3.2T NPO
optical engine demand across China and overseas markets. Nevertheless, equipment

suppliers remain the earliest beneficiary in CPO, with the strong testing and coupling
demand reflecting in equipment suppliers’ revenues in mid 2026. For example,
Robotechnik 2Q26 revenues were up 172% QoQ, or 141% higher than our estimates. As
we highlighted in Sep (report link), we estimate CPO switch at 10k / 92k / 131k units in
2026E-28E.
5. AI spending sustainability: Management highlighted that AI is a revolution, and we
are at an early stage of infrastructure spending and application development. It will be a

long cycle for the coming 10 to 20 years, and some of their customers are planning
beyond 2030. The demand for the coming 2 to 5 years remains very strong and
management highlighted their industry enjoys short return on investment, which is
around 1 to 2 years. Management highlighted the AI spending is not only building more
AI data centers, but also includes technology migration, such as making power
consumption more efficient, making data transmission faster, etc., driving the supply

chain growth. Compared to the internet bubble in the 2000s, management sees much
more transparency between end-customers (demand) and supply chain (supply), as end
customers are involved more in supply chain product development and capacity
investment, reducing the information gap between demand and supply, and reducing
the chance of double booking. Management noted that currently every module they
produce is being deployed in data centers, reflecting real demand.

6. Photonics IC scaling up: AI drives data transmission speed and bandwidth
requirement, leading to strong demand on Photonics IC, requesting it to catch up
Electrical IC automated production and testing ecosystem in 1 to 2 years, while EIC /
semiconductors has been developed for decades. The strong demand brings
opportunities to tool suppliers, across coupling and testing, which are necessary tools to
accelerate PIC production. Robotechnik / FiconTEC describes the number of tools they

target to ship in the coming 1 year would be over 50% of what they’ve shipped in the
past 25 years, reflecting the strong demand and orders visibility.
14 September 2026   2
Goldman SachsFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

7. Testing - CPO earliest beneficiary: As a new technology, CPO naturally takes time
and faces challenges in the path of commercialization; however, we identify testing
equipment suppliers as the earliest beneficiaries, considering the expensive final module
means multiple testing during the production process is necessary, given the earlier a
failure is found, the lower cost a failure is. As a result, companies we visited continue to
see Insertion 2 (double-sided wafer-level testing) as necessary, and Robotechnik /

FiconTEC launched the new generation in 3Q26, which fully complied with the global
leading foundry’s communication infrastructure.
8. Testing - shortening time with KGD (know good die) at early stage: To facilitate
photonics IC scaling up, not only are more tools needed but better tools, requiring
suppliers’ strong R&D and execution, delivering continuous specification upgrade.
Robotechnik / FiconTEC, as global leaders in PIC testing, aim to shorten the wafer-level

testing time by 50-60% next year, and target 4x faster die-level testing next year.
Enlitech (7728.TWO, Not Covered) offers NightJar, a hyperspectral image system,
revealing AWG (arrayed waveguide grating) optical loss pathways with absolute optical
power mapping, allowing customers to KGD at Insertion 1 (PIC wafer testing) or Insertion
0 (PIC wafer early defect screening), saving failure costs.
9. Coupling - automated production becoming necessary in high-speed connection:

AI drives optical module towards high speed and high density / bandwidth, driving
demand on automated production. GMT (4573.TWO, not covered) showcased dual FA
active alignment equipment for 1.6T optical module, which could do the coupling for
both Tx and Rx simultaneously, saving 50% of coupling time compared to traditional
equipment. FitTech (6706.TW, not covered) has showcased an optical engine assembly
system, featuring passive / active alignment and customized development of coupling

algorithms, which could be used in multiple products: high-speed optical modules, ELS,
OCS, NPO, CPO, SiPh and optical chiplets. ASMPT has showcased MEGA multi-chip
bonding solution for complex multi-chip and photonics packaging applications,
combining high-precision placement with adhesive dispensing, UV fixation and 3D
inspection in one platform.
10. FAU & Laser specification upgrade: With optical networking expanding from scale
out to scale up, FAU and laser specification is also migrating. FOCI‘s FAU in 2026 to be

mainly 40 channels (starting in 3Q26) for 3.2T, followed by 80 channels (within 2026) for
6.4T, and 100 channels under R&D, per management. The company’s FAU enjoys several
customers across fabless, foundry, laser suppliers, etc. FOCI Aug revenues accelerated to
+20% MoM (vs. +9% MoM in Jul), reflecting the ramp up of high-end FAU. VPEC‘s
optoelectronics revenues enjoy strong growth on the back of strong CW lasers demand
and InP substrate supply getting better. Its InP epiwafer for CW lasers is also migrating

from 70mW / 100mW (scale out) to above 100mW (scale up, NPO) and 300mW /
400mW (scale up, CPO), along with expansion from CW lasers to 100G / 200G EML,
supporting its growth ahead.
14 September 2026   3
Goldman SachsFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Zhongji Innolight Co.
Valuation: Our 12-m TP for Innolight A-share of Rmb2,645 is based on 35.6x 2027E P/E,
and our 12-m TP for Innolight H-share of HK$3,267 is based on a H-A premium of 13%.
We derive our target P/E multiple from the networking peers’ trading P/E vs.
forward-year fundamentals (NI YoY and OPM). Compared to Innolight’s historical trading
range, the target multiple reflects our view of its leading market position in high-speed

connections and product-mix upgrade toward SiPh optical modules, driving GM
expansion. We are Buy rated.
Key downside risks: 1) slower-/worse-than-expected 800G+ optical modules demand;
2) slower/worse-than-expected new optical devices ramp pace; 3)
faster-than-expected market share normalizing in 800G+ optical modules; 4)
geopolitical risks; and 5) worsening component supply situation to constrain shipment
growth.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC

Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is
based on a 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics
supply chain.
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - RoboTechnik
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of Rmb796 is based on a 20.7x 2031E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 11.3%. Our target multiple is derived from the
correlation between consumer electronic peers’ PEG&M (2026E P/E to 2027E NI YoY and
OPM), with RoboTechnik’s 2032E net income at 2% YoY and 2032E OPM at 32%.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition; lower-than-expected

optical connection end demand; worse-than-expected photovoltaic market downcycle.
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of NT$833 is based on a 15.4x 2030E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 12.6%. Our target multiple is derived from peers’
correlation between forward-year trading P/E and earnings growth.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition, technology migration
trends in optical networking, and slower-than-expected capacity ramp-up.

Price target methodology and risks - ASMPT
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral rated on ASMPT with a 12-month target price
of HK$177, which is based on a 24.9x 2030E discounted P/E (discounted back to 2027E).
Our target P/E is derived from correlation between peers P/E to the sum of NI YoY and
OPM.
Key upside/downside risks: 1) customers’ faster-/slower-than-expected adoption of
Advanced Packaging tools; 2) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand from

automotive customers; and 3) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for traditional
IC packaging and SMT equipment.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 1

We hosted TECO (1504.TW, not covered) management at our Asia Leaders 
Conference 2026 (Aug 31 - Sep 2) in Hong Kong. Management is positive on their 
data center business, which includes construction services, products (e.g. 
transformer, busway, AC motor controls, etc.), and modular data center (MDC). 
Management remains positive on data center demand in Southeast Asia, highlighting 
a major CSP client’s 1GW (5-year) plan in Thailand, Malaysia, and potential in 
Indonesia in the future. The positive tone echoes our positive view on the AI 
infrastructure cycle, on which we raised our AI servers implied AI chips forecast by 
14% / 22% / 14% in 2026E-28E in June (report link) to +75% / 42% / 20% YoY in 
2026E-28E, or 19m / 27m / 32m in 2026E-28E. Capex estimates on leading US CSPs 
was also raised by 8% / 27% / 25% in 2026E-28E to +76% / +35% / +8% YoY in 
2026E-28E, or up to US$1.1tn by 2028E (report link). As we highlighted in our FII 
Chairman and Innolight Chairman visits takeaways, supply chain remains positive on 
rising AI investments in coming years, with spending across CSP , NeoCloud, 
Government, and Enterprise, and we are at an early stage of infrastructure spending 
and application development. End-customers are involved more in supply chain 
product development and capacity investment, also better reflecting real demand.  
Company profile: TECO (1504.TW, not covered) was founded in 1956, starting with 
motor manufacturing and is currently among the five largest industrial motor 
suppliers worldwide. The company mainly offers electromechanical systems, with 
three major business groups of Green Mechatronic Solutions, Intelligence Energy, 
and Air & Intelligent Life. Apart from its traditional motor business, TECO is 
expanding in new markets including ESCO low-carbon solutions, EV powertrain, 
offshore wind power, energy storage system and data center engineering. The 
company has built 700 MV of data centers in Southeast Asia. 
Key Takeaways

1. Data center business: Management remains positive on the business and stated 
they provide both construction services across power, cooling, and networking, and

### Page 2

related products (e.g. transformer, busway, AC motor controls, etc.). The company’s data
center business focuses on Southeast Asia, where a major customer (a leading US CSP)
announced a 1GW data center in Malaysia (announced in 2025), 1GW data center in
Thailand (announced in 2026), and potential new data center in Indonesia. Other than
leading US CSPs, the company also sees opportunities in sovereign AI, for example,
Japan is planning a 5-year 350mW AI data center, which could use MDC (modular data

center) for speedy deployment.
2. Modular Data Center (MDC) strength: Management highlights MDC saves AI data
center deployment time by ~50% to within 9 months, and reducing 15-20% capex, as it
moves most work into factory production, reducing onsite engineering. Modular Data
Center includes: modular IT server system, modular UPS system, modular power
distribution system, modular container genset system (power generation), and modular
cooling system, facilitating the AI data center deployment. The company’s product in

Malaysia is also for a leading US CSP , which one MDC could deliver 2.7mW.

### Page 1

UMT provides rectangular waveguides, which are conducting pipes with a 
rectangular cross-section used to guide the propagation of microwave or mmwave 
signals. Different shapes and combinations of waveguides can form various RF 
passive components, such as filter / diplexer, coupler, isolator, antenna, power 
amplifiers (PAs), etc. The company serves leading global LEO satellite operators, and 
its waveguides can be seen in LEO satellites / payloads (main), and gateways in 
ground stations.

### Page 2

LEO satellite launches update: Starlink launched 261 LEO satellites in Aug 2026, or 
+13% MoM. The number of satellites per launch continues to increase from 24 satellites 
per launch in May 2026 to 26 satellites per launch in Aug 2026, and at 27 satellites per 
launch so far in Sep 2026, reaffirming our positive view on the constellation construction 
cycle. On Sep 18, SpaceX’s Starship V3 is scheduled to have the 3rd flight and the first 
Starship flight to deploy satellites (a group of Starlink satellites) into stable LEO, and 
should have the 4th flight of Starship V3 within 2026 (link). Starship V3 is a reusable 
vehicle, with LEO capacity at 100k kg, or 339% higher than Falcon 9 Block 5 (22.8kg), the 
current vehicle that Starlink is using. The vehicle migration would not only accelerate 
Starlink’s satellite launches but also release the legacy vehicle (e.g. Falcon 9 Block 5) to 
other LEO satellite operators, facilitating the overall industry’s satellite launches. For 
example, Amazon has planned 10 launches (LF-04 to LF-13) in 2029, using Falcon 9 
Block 5. Assuming 27 satellites per launch via the vehicle, the 10 missions would bring 
270 satellites (vs. 180 / 216 satellites in 2025 / 7M26 that Amazon added). Amazon is 
also increasing its satellites per launch: the upcoming pipeline shows 35-48 satellites per 
launch vs. 29-36 satellites per launch in May to July 2026, or increasing by 28% on avg. 
The acceleration of LEO satellite launches reaffirms our positive view on UMT.

Earnings revision: We factor in Aug revenues, and raise net income by 1% in 2026-28E. 
The revenue raise is to reflect our positive view on accelerating LEO satellite launches 
along with specification upgrade driving up the dollar content. Our GM is reduced to 
reflect our expectation that the larger scale would lead to lower GM with customers’ 
rising procurement power. Nevertheless, with the company’s product mix upgrade 
toward LEO satellite, the blended GM should continue to increase through 2028E.

### Page 3

Compared to Bloomberg consensus, our net income is in line in 2026E and 13% higher in 
2027E, reflecting our positive view on the LEO satellite constellation construction cycle, 
UMT’s dollar content increase on specification upgrade, and UMT’s leading market 
position considering its strong design capability, commitment in capacity expansion and 
timely services to customers. In Aug, the company announced investment of US$12m 
(NT$388m) in Vietnam and procured US$9.3m of equipment for its Vietnam factory, 
reflecting its continuous capacity expansion and diversification. The company also 
announced it would set up a subsidiary in the US with investment of up to US$10m 
(NT$323m), and we expect the subsidiary could support the company to serve its major 
customers in a timely manner.

### Page 4

Valuation: We continue to derive our target price based on a discounted P/E, and based 
on 2029E (methodology unchanged). We continue to derive the target P/E multiple from 
the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the satellite supply chain, and we remove outliers 
(those below 0.3x, which are mostly telecom operators who face competition from LEO 
satellite operators or having consumer electronics exposure which suffered from high 
memory costs). Our target 2029E discounted P/E multiple is at 37.0x (unchanged) which 
is based on peers’ avg. PEG&M, and UMT’s 2029-30E avg. NI YoY and OPM. We apply the 
37.0x multiple to 2029E EPS and discount it back to 2027E via a COE of 7.8% (no 
change). Our target price is at NT$2,557 (vs. NT$2,513 previously), implying 61x 2027E 
P/E, which is above the company’s average +1 stv. P/E of 42x, reflecting our positive view 
on UMT’s product mix upgrade toward LEO satellites. Maintain Buy.

### Page 5

Price target risks and methodology - UMT 
Valuation: We use a discounted P/E methodology and apply a 37.0x target P/E multiple 
to UMT’s 2029E EPS, discounting it back to 2027E at a COE of 7.8% (beta 1.2x, risk-free 
rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), which leads to our 12-month target price 
of NT$2,557. The target P/E is based on the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the 
satellite supply chain. We are Buy-rated on UMT.  
Key Risks: Slower-than-expected LEO satellite deployment; Potential competition from 
new-entrant suppliers; LEO satellite operators manufacturing in-house components.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
